<div class="index-section no-main-image page alt-section" data-collection-id="5ba16b6d70a6adc8d825cf73" data-edit-main-image="Background" data-url-id="core-features" id="core-features-section">
<div class="index-section-wrapper">
<!-- For Pages -->
<div class="content page-content" data-url-id="core-features" id="core-features-page">
<div class="content-inner has-content">
<div class="sqs-layout sqs-grid-12 columns-12" data-type="page" data-updated-on="1570219041565" id="page-5ba16b6d70a6adc8d825cf73"><div class="row sqs-row"><div class="col sqs-col-12 span-12"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1539873079924_10797"><div class="sqs-block-content"><h2 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Packed with features that make running your business easier.</h2><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>(and we keep building more)</em></p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Client &amp; Pet Management</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Unlimited profiles.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Upload photos of owners and pets.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Go paperless with digital waivers/agreements.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Store vaccination records, track expiration-dates, automate reminders/notifications.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Track daycare and boarding package credits.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Track account balances.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Manage reservations, appointments and deposits right from the owner- and pet-profile.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Track all transaction history and details.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Record ‘hidden’ employee notes about the owner and/or pet.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Use Custom Icons (with super-powers!) to easily communicate pet-care needs and details to your staff.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Track calls, emails and SMS (2-way SMS add-on).</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Assign bar-codes to owners for quick check-in.</p></li></ul><p class="" data-rte-preserve-empty="true" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"></p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Online Booking / Customer-Portal</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>FACT: Gingr customers save $100s-$1000s per month in staff-wages with Gingr’s online customer-portal. </strong></p><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Branded with your logo, optional custom js and css.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customizable lead forms, customer self-registration, pet- and reservation-forms.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Mobile-friendly, screen-responsive. No native-app required.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Easily link from your web-page, social media and emails. Or embed/i-frame in your website.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customers can sign waivers/agreements online.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customers can upload files, vet-records.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customers can view photos, videos and web-cams (with iDogCam integration).</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customers can <em>request</em> boarding or daycare reservations, grooming appointments, and request enrollment in group classes.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customize what services are available on-line, what are only available through the business.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Recurring/repeat appointments or reservations.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customize what forms/fields are required for certain reservation-types.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Availability calendar settings.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Up-sell additional activities and services.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Sell packages online (Day Services and Resort-tiers only).</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Collect deposits online (with integrated card processing)</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Embed contact us forms directly on your website that feed back to Gingr.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.gingrapp.com/contact-us" target="_blank">Contact us</a> to learn more about Gingr’s online booking features!</p></li></ul><p class="" data-rte-preserve-empty="true" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"></p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Dashboard: Check-Ins/Outs, Reservation Management</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Quick view of all pets Expected, Going Home and Checked In, reservation Requests.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Search/filter by reservation/appointment-type, custom icons.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Easy view of add-on services. Add new services, mark as complete.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">1-click Quick-check-in for reservations.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Quick-Check-in  multiple pets from multiple owners.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Quick check in multiple pets from multiple owners at once.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Check-in pop-up warnings for expired vaccinations, unsigned agreements, custom icons.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Check-in form for boarding reservations to ensure you have all the details.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Quick check-out to point-of-sale, payment collection.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Quickly create ‘report cards’ to send out photos/videos and notes to customers.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Post and schedule staff Notices.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Quick navigation to all features/pages, calendars.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Quick login/out for employee-users/permissions.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Quick staff clock-in using TimeClock.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customize tabs and quick-links/bookmarks.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Reservation/appointment summary widgets.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Local-weather widget.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Print reservations reports right from the dashboard.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a customer-portal, what is ‘staff-only’ scheduling.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Capacity-group settings.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Automatically convert from one reservation-type to another (i.e, half-day daycare to full-day).</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Automatic email and SMS confirmations and reminders.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Customize email text for each reservation/service-type.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">‘Require completed reservation prior to this reservation-type’ settings.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Advanced pricing-rule / rate-modifier settings (2nd pet rate, multi-night rates, VIP, etc).</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Add-on services settings to each reservation-type, with frequency options, scheduling, automatic pricing.</p></li><li><p class="" and Resort-tiers only)</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Sell pre-paid packages (aka ‘punchcards’) for daycare, boarding, services.</p></li><li><p class="" </h1></div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1521588065368_9719"><div class="sqs-block-content"><h3 style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;">Get Gingr for as low as $2.58/day</h3></div></div><div class="sqs-block code-block sqs-block-code" data-block-type="23" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1521215475812_30657"><div class="sqs-block-content"><script src="//www.powr.io/powr.js?external-type=squarespace"></script>

Unlimited up to 1000 reply texts/mo (covers 99% of our customers). $.02 after.</p><p class="" data-rte-preserve-empty="true" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"></p><p class="" style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Your Gingr subscription will also earn you discounts with several of our industry partners</strong>, including <a href="https://broadly.com/partner/gingr" target="_blank">Broadly</a> and <a href="https://paccert.org/" target="_blank">PACCC</a>.</p></div></div><div class="sqs-block image-block sqs-block-image sqs-text-ready" data-aspect-ratio="96.5" data-block-type="5" id="block-yui_3_17_2_4_1516171944943_10049"><div class="sqs-block-content" id="yui_3_17_2_1_1571870341194_214">
